# Exhaustive Search: ALL Stores to 0x831C0000-0x831CFFFF Region

Generated by `find_all_831C_stores_v2.py` with register-tracking analysis.

## Method

Scanned all 77 generated `.cpp` files, tracking register values through:
- `lis` (load immediate shifted): `ctx.rN.s64 = LITERAL`
- `addi` (add immediate): `ctx.rN.s64 = ctx.rM.s64 + OFFSET`
- `mr` (move register): `ctx.rN.u64 = ctx.rM.u64`

For each `PPC_STORE_U*` call, resolved the address register to a concrete value
and checked if it falls in 0x831C0000-0x831CFFFF.

|-|-|
| Generated stores to 0x831C region | 1516 |
| Functions with stores | 478 |
| Stores to exactly 0x831C2458 | 0 |
| Hook file stores to 0x831C | 1 |

## Conclusion

**CONFIRMED: No generated recomp function writes to 0x831C2458 (scene list pointer).**

However, 1516 stores target other addresses in the 0x831C region across 478 functions. These write to nearby globals (render state, device flags, etc.) but none touches the scene list pointer slot at offset 9304.

The scene list pointer must be populated by a hook. The original writer was in the Xbox 360 D3D kernel device initialization path, which is excluded from recompilation.
